## FC-51 A publish-path change silently dropped a field a later gate depends on (PROMOTE-GITHEAD)
- User saw: 11.3.1 stuck on the npm `next` tag; Promote run 37784566223 failed "FATAL: loki-mode@11.3.1 has no gitHead on npm", so the release never reached latest, Docker latest or Homebrew. Every later release would stick the same way.
- Law: evidence or it did not happen; a change to one stage must keep every field a downstream stage reads.
- Cause (measured): D90 (6247695f5) changed release.yml to `npm pack` then `npm publish <tgz>`. A tarball publish records no gitHead (only a directory publish reads .git); 11.3.0 and 11.2.0 have it, 11.3.1 does not. promote.yml requires gitHead to prove the build is an ancestor of origin/main.
- Siblings swept: grep of .github/workflows and scripts for gitHead finds only promote.yml (reader). The other publish in release.yml (loki-mode-sdk, `npm publish` from sdk/typescript, a directory publish) still records gitHead and promote does not read it, so it is unaffected. sbom.yml packs but does not publish.
- Mechanism: (1) release.yml stamps `git rev-parse HEAD` into the manifest before `npm pack`, restores package.json, and asserts the packed manifest gitHead equals HEAD. (2) promote.yml, when npm has no gitHead, falls back to the commit of tag v${VERSION} only if the tag is annotated (release.yml creates it with `git tag -a`), that commit has a successful Tests run on main, and the published tarball's loki-ts/dist/loki.js embeds the version; the source used is logged as GITHEAD_SOURCE. The 40-hex, ancestor-of-origin/main and never-move-latest-backwards checks apply to both paths unchanged.
- Fixture: tests/test-promote-head-stamp.sh (static check of both workflow definitions plus 8 negative mutations, one per required piece). Red on a0d599d0c (11 requirement failures), green after.

## FC-53 A host-dependent or pipe-dependent assertion reddens the nightly on one OS (NIGHTLY-FLAKE)
- User saw: Nightly 37790534786 (a0d599d0c) red, blocking releases under D90. Raw: Bun macOS (both bun versions) `(fail) gate-failures cap head/tail parity (W4 L1) > doctor per-provider install hint goes to STDERR` "Expected: <= 1, Received: 2" at gate-failures-cap-parity.test.ts:81; and Full suite Shell shard 1/8 `TEST 1: basic onboard on loki-mode repo ... FAIL` after `echo: write error: Broken pipe`.
- Law: evidence or it did not happen; an assertion must test the property it names, not a host side effect; never retry, lengthen a sleep or skip to turn a gate green.
- Cause (measured): (1) The doctor test counted any stdout line containing "Install:". On macOS the system python3 is 3.9, so doctor prints a Python 3.12 warning ending "Install: brew install python@3.12", a second match. It failed on the same SHA in the dispatched run 37780519439 too (job 113322165616, same line), so it was never flaky: deterministic on macOS, hidden because that run's overall verdict was success. (2) `loki onboard` ran `find ... | wc -l` under `set -euo pipefail` (autonomy/loki cmd_onboard); when find exits non-zero on an unreadable or concurrently vanishing entry (other suites create and delete temp files in the repo root during shard runs) the whole command died silently, rc 1 and 0 bytes. Reproduced locally under 14-way parallel load, trace ends at the find|wc line; the same suite passed in run 37780519439 (job 113322165426). In the log the test also saw `echo: write error: Broken pipe` because test-onboard-command.sh used `echo "$output" | grep -q` under pipefail on a 37KB report.
- Siblings swept: all 11 `echo "$output" | grep -q` sites in test-onboard-command.sh converted. Other suites use the same idiom (grep over tests/*.sh finds it in ~100 files) but only misfire when the producer output exceeds the pipe buffer; none of those showed a nightly failure, so they are tracked by the flake tracker rather than bulk-edited. Bun tests: the only bare "Install:" counter in loki-ts/tests was the doctor test.
- Mechanism: (1) the doctor test counts only lines carrying a provider install command and requires the sole allowed one to be the claude fallback. (2) Product fix: the two find/grep count pipelines in cmd_onboard tolerate a non-zero producer (`|| true`, the count still comes from wc). Test fix: grep reads the variable through a here-string, which has no upstream writer in the pipeline status. Shared guard: scripts/nightly-flake-tracker.sh compares saved job logs per SHA and emits FLIP (pass in one run, fail in another on one SHA, with a BOARD-ready line) or STABLE-FAIL (red in every run, a real defect).
- Fixture: test-onboard-command.sh TEST 11 (unreadable directory makes find exit 1; red on the unguarded line with rc 1, green after) and tests/test-nightly-flake-tracker.sh (fixtures in tests/fixtures/nightly-flake). Replay of the real logs: `nightly-flake-tracker.sh SHA:a:<job 113356420172 log> SHA:b:<job 113322165616 log>` reports the doctor test as STABLE-FAIL.

- Cause (measured): `git diff --name-only 54a519a22 eee891534` lists 21 paths; four were missing from ALLOWLIST (helm/loki-mode/Chart.yaml, deploy/helm/autonomi/Chart.yaml, deploy/helm/control-plane/Chart.yaml, docs/CLI-REFERENCE.md), all written by scripts/generate-stale-zero.sh, which bump_all_version_files runs (SZ-01) after the allowlist was last edited. Because test.yml's gate uses the same allowlist, its heavy jobs also ran at the bump SHA, and that completed failure then beat the parent reuse (has_conclusion failure is checked before parent reuse, by design: only a missing/cancelled run falls through).
- Siblings swept: release.sh bump_all_version_files slots and generate-stale-zero.sh targets (README.md facts block carries no version and is unchanged by a bump; SKILL.md, Chart.yaml appVersion, CLI-REFERENCE.md generated line are the only generated version carriers). Both copies of the allowlist (release.yml STEP 1, scripts/ci/version-bump-only.sh) updated; they are byte-compared by tests/test-version-bump-only.sh.
- Mechanism: one derivation, not two lists. tests/test-version-bump-only.sh now derives the version-bearing paths from scripts/release.sh (update_version_slot targets) and scripts/generate-stale-zero.sh (Chart.yaml and CLI-REFERENCE targets) and asserts each is in the allowlist of both copies.
- Fixture: tests/test-version-bump-only.sh "FC-70" cases (static subset check, per-file chart/cli-ref pure-bump fixtures, and a replay of 54a519a22 -> eee891534 when both objects exist). Red before the fix (ALLOWLIST lacks the four paths), green after.

## FC-69 FORCE_COLOR in the user's env colored the test output and the Wall base run read no counts (WALL-COLOR)
- User saw: with FORCE_COLOR=3 (node v26) `loki start "sum() skips the first element; fix it" --no-pr` sealed NOT PROVEN "wall base run not_run: 1, wall test discarded" with 1 executed check instead of 2. - Mechanism: util/check_result.ts plainTestEnv() (FORCE_COLOR=0, NO_COLOR=1, CLICOLOR_FORCE removed, CI untouched) for every harness-run test or verify child, no per-runner special case; the single exported stripAnsi() at capture and at classify entry (belt and braces). stages/seal.ts names every not_run Wall file as "Wall test not executed: <file>: <reason>" on the receipt and the console NOT PROVEN line. ## FC-55 Intake discovery session retried or hung on a repo with nothing to discover (INTAKE-FAST)
- User saw: intake taking 10-44s (median 28s) on a trivial repo, one replay hanging 120s, before any work began. Raw: the Project Model discovery session is almost all of intake; a no-UI repo answers `ui: {present: false, cite: []}` and validateAnswer rejects it with "packages[0].ui.cite: needs at least one citation", which spends the one retry (a second full session).
- Law: evidence or it did not happen; a validator may demand evidence only for a claim the answer makes; a stage must be bounded by a recorded fallback, never a stall.
- Cause (measured): schema.ts demanded a non-empty ui.cite whether or not ui.present was true. Separately, a repo whose every tracked file is at the root has no package below the root to learn, yet still paid a full session, and nothing bounded a session that never returned.
- Siblings swept: every other optional field in validateAnswer (commands.* null, ui.boot null, install, dependsOn) already accepts its empty form; ui.cite was the only citation demanded for an absent claim. Consumers of an unknown model (loadProjectApi in verify, wall packageOf, implement, run_cap, visual evidence, testmap, before_after) all resolve to null and keep repo-root behavior; commandFor takes its runner command from the harness, and a root-only model never changes it.
- Mechanism: (1) ui.cite is checked only when ui.present is true (a supplied cite is still verified). (2) discoverProjectModel returns unknownModel("", "single-directory repo") with no session when shallowDirs is exactly ["."], after the committed and cached checks. (3) Each discovery session is raced against its limit plus 5s; a hang aborts the session, emits `project_model.fallback`, and returns an unknown model with owner=provider and no retry. Session ceiling 120s to 60s.
- Fixture: loki-ts/tests/project_model/intake_fast.test.ts. Red on origin/main (3 of 7 fail incl. the hang case), green after.

## FC-82 A live-server E2E suite failed collection on a CI runner without its client libraries (RED-PURPLE)
- User saw: Tests red on main 082cd57a4 (run 37848611813, shard 4): `FAIL rc=2: py_test tests/docker/test_purple_lab.py`, `ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'requests'`.
- Law: a test needing an optional dependency or a live service must skip honestly, never error at collection (same family as FC-73 baseline rule).
- Cause (measured): the Purple Lab server still exists (web-app/server.py, autonomy/loki 'loki web', deprecated v7.44.0), so the suite is not stale. It hit `import requests` at module top on a runner without requests, and would also need a server on localhost:57375 that CI never starts.
- Siblings swept: tests/docker/test_polling.py imports checked, no unguarded optional import of this kind.
- Mechanism: pytest.importorskip for pytest_asyncio, requests, websockets plus a module-level skip when BASE_URL is unreachable.
- Fixture: `python3 -m pytest -q tests/docker/test_purple_lab.py` with no server exits 0 reporting a skip; before the fix it exited 2 on a runner without requests.
- r2 (main 4511d9f32): the module-level skip (importorskip / skip(allow_module_level=True)) collected zero tests, so pytest exited rc=5 (no tests collected) and the fast gate reported `FAIL rc=5: py_test`. The skip is now per-test: imports are guarded with try/except, the module collects all tests, and a pytestmark skipif (same reasons) skips each one, giving N skipped with rc=0. Assertions are unchanged and the tests run when deps and server are present.

## FC-86 A plan far above the shard cap overflowed every shard with no fail-safe (PLAN-OVERFLOW)
- User saw: main red. Tests runs 37852209020 (4f8b6386b) and 37851435720 (4511d9f32) lost "Impacted suites" shards 6, 8 and 10 to the job's timeout-minutes: 6 ("The operation was canceled"); shard 8 was about 60% through its pytest run. The plan log read "fast-gate: base=v11.3.2 head=HEAD files=85 planned=1086".
- Law: a bounded resource needs a fail-safe path when the input exceeds the bound; an over-capacity plan must go FULL, never be packed into shards that cannot finish.
- Cause (measured): the packer sets n = min(MAX_SHARDS=12, ceil(total/SHARD_TARGET_S=60)). When total is far above 12*60s the min silently clamps n, so every shard carries more than the target and overflows the 6 minute job cap. Nothing in the plan path checked the clamp.
- Siblings swept: the other plan bounds each already have a FULL row (unparseable-diff, empty-diff, too-many-files at 150, selector-failed, selector-answered-R0, global-guards-unreadable) and the plan step timeout forces full=true on a nonzero rc; the shard count was the one bounded quantity without one.
- Mechanism: in the python packer, ceil(total/target) > maxs writes the single row "FULL\tplan-over-capacity\t0\t0" (write_matrix then emits full=true and full-suite.yml runs) and prints total, target and maxs on stderr. Timeouts, MAX_SHARDS and SHARD_TARGET_S defaults are unchanged.
- Fixture: tests/test-fast-gate.sh PLAN-OVERFLOW (FAST_GATE_MAX_SHARDS=1, FAST_GATE_SHARD_TARGET_S=1 gives FULL and full=true; default env on the same diff gives no FULL).

- User saw: main red. Full suite / Bun tests failed loki-ts/tests/util/full_env_spawn_guard.test.ts on 9fcd59459 with mismatched [runner/load_owner.ts, engine10/stages/verify.ts, engine10/stages/wall.ts].

Not fixed here; a follow-up slice should make a loki-ts/src change select the walk-all-src guard tests.

## FC-89 A guard that walks all of loki-ts/src names no stem, so the fast gate never selected it (FASTGATE-GLOBAL-GUARDS)
- User saw: WALL-COLOR (FC-69) reached main red; the diff-selected fast gate ran only a typecheck. Third miss of the CTO rule that global guards always run.
- Law: a guard whose scope is the whole tree must be selected by what it is, not by which file changed; a selection keyed on a name in the test text cannot see a test that names nothing.

- Siblings swept: every loki-ts/tests/**/*.test.ts that walks a src-rooted path (17 found, listed by the marker); three false positives that only walk tmp or tests dirs carry the audited opt-out.
- Mechanism: a test that walks the src tree carries the line "// select: walk-all-src". scripts/select-tests.sh emit_marked_walkers finds them by content and emits each as an R8 row on every diff (skipping any already covered by a global-guards.tsv row or directory row), so a new walker needs no list edit.

## FC-90 Deleting the token env vars left the user's gh login and git credential helpers reachable (GH-KEYRING)
- User saw: nothing yet (found by audit). With GH_TOKEN, GITHUB_TOKEN, GH_ENTERPRISE_TOKEN, GITHUB_ENTERPRISE_TOKEN and SSH_AUTH_SOCK removed, `gh auth token` exited 0 and `gh api user` returned the user's login on the founder machine, and git held `osxkeychain` plus `!gh auth git-credential` helpers over an HTTPS origin.
- Law: a child spawned by the harness must not reach the user's GitHub credential by default or by accident, through any store the child's env can resolve (env vars, gh config and keyring, git credential helpers), not through the env vars alone; one shared mechanism builds the withheld env, so no env builder can delete a subset and call it token-free. This is not a sandbox.
- Cause (measured): tokenFreeEnv (util/safe_git.ts) and plainTestEnv (util/check_result.ts) only deleted the vars. gh then falls back to GH_CONFIG_DIR/hosts.yml and the OS keyring, and git calls its configured helpers on an HTTPS push. withholdGithubTokens already closed this for the worker, but only for the worker env. Measured with gh: GH_CONFIG_DIR alone leaves `gh auth token` exit 0 (keyring); a sentinel token alone makes `gh auth status` fail but a named-account read (`gh auth token --user`) still reads hosts.yml; both together close it.
- Mechanism: util/credential_env.ts hardenCredentialEnv is the one function: the 4 vars become a non-working sentinel, GH_CONFIG_DIR is a per-process empty mode-700 directory, credential.helper is reset through GIT_CONFIG_COUNT/KEY/VALUE appended after existing entries (the user's gitconfig is never edited and identity survives), GIT_TERMINAL_PROMPT=0 and GCM_INTERACTIVE=never. tokenFreeEnv (so plainTestEnv, safeGitEnv) and withholdGithubTokens both call it; the LOKI_ALLOW_AGENT_GITHUB_TOKEN opt-out stays in withholdGithubTokens only, test envs are always hardened. The empty dir is made once per process (not per call), under LOKI_RUN_TMP when it is a real directory, else the OS temp dir, and removed by exact path at exit (the old per-call scoped dirs leaked).
- Siblings swept: withholdGithubTokens (now shared), tokenFreeEnv callers (attempts, wall_snapshot, verify_pr, start), plainTestEnv callers (wall, verify, load_owner), the worker assertion (sentinel prefix now exported from credential_env.ts), and the bash route (_loki_withhold_github_tokens at autonomy/run.sh main() already sets the sentinel, a scoped GH_CONFIG_DIR, the helper reset and an EXIT trap removal). Raw `process.env` spawns elsewhere inherit an already-withheld process env (runner after autonomous.ts withholdGithubTokens, worker after supervisor.ts) and are policed by full_env_spawn_guard.
- Fixture: tests/util/fc90_credential_env.test.ts (a fake HOME with a planted gh hosts.yml and a planted global git credential helper, each with a positive control on the raw env; red on the old tokenFreeEnv, red when the GH_CONFIG_DIR line, the credential.helper reset or the sentinel is removed; plus a count of loki-gh-config-* dirs in an isolated TMPDIR before and after).
- CI note (fix-forward, cc8e17512 red on ubuntu): the named-account control `gh auth token --user` returned empty on gh for Linux because a flat hosts.yml is not read by a named lookup there (the multi-account `users:` shape is), while the default `gh auth token` reads only the flat one on the same gh. The fixture now probes the installed gh with each shape in a scratch home, plants the one that works in a separate named home, and skips the named-account test (control and hardened assertion together) when no shape works. Verified on ubuntu:24.04 with gh 2.102.0.
- Residual: code running as the same user can unset the variables and call gh or git directly (or read the keyring itself); this closes default and accidental reach, it is not a sandbox. A parent GIT_CONFIG_PARAMETERS helper is neutralised by appending the reset last. SSH keys under ~/.ssh and GIT_SSH_COMMAND are closed only for the worker env (withholdGithubTokens), not for plainTestEnv; a test command can still `ssh` with the user's default keys.

## FC-88 The release gate repeated the Tests workflow's typecheck and bun suite on the same tree (WF-GATE-DEDUP)
- User saw: every release waited 6m40s in release.yml's gate step "Bun typecheck + test (protect published loki-ts dist)" (run 37859034694, v11.3.4) for work the Tests workflow had already finished green on the same tree, or on the bump-only parent (FC-70).
- Law: a gate does only what the verdict it depends on cannot; a second copy of a check is latency with no added safety, and it hides which check is the real one.
- Cause (measured): the step ran `bun install`, `bun run typecheck` and the full `bun test` plus a Python and pytest setup for the Wall tests. Tests already runs tsc (test.yml "Typecheck loki-ts (tsc)", lines 193-195) and the bun suites (impacted shards, full-suite fallback), and `release` needs required-ci (release.yml, `needs: [gate, required-ci]`), whose REQUIRED list names Tests and accepts the parent verdict for a bump-only commit. The one thing Tests cannot see was never checked in the gate: that the committed dist is a fresh build of the release SHA and that the packed tarball carries it.
- Siblings swept: publish-npm's pack check (release.yml "Pack once and verify the PACKED tarball", it also rebuilds through prepack; untouched), local-ci.sh's own typecheck and bun test (local tier, not a CI gate; untouched), version-bump-only reuse (unchanged), test.yml typecheck-build (kept as the single typecheck).
- Mechanism: scripts/ci/verify-release-dist.sh. `build` rebuilds loki-ts/dist and compares all four files to the committed dist byte for byte, ignoring only the debugId; `tarball` does the same for the dist inside `npm pack --ignore-scripts`. The gate runs these two and nothing that Tests runs.
- Fixture: tests/test-release-gate-dedup.sh (red when the gate reintroduces bun test, pytest or typecheck, loses either dist check, when release stops needing required-ci, or when required-ci stops requiring Tests; plus verifier behavior on a debugId-only tarball and a real byte difference).

## FC-99 Worktree sprawl exhausted the disk and stalled builds (AUTO-REAP)
- User saw: .claude/worktrees grew to 377 worktrees / 56G (cap 15) on the founder's laptop; the volume hit 96% full and ENOSPC stalled builds.
- Law: whatever creates a resource owns its removal; a count cap that only reports (WORKTREE_COUNT at 15) is a control nobody acts on.
- Cause (measured): slice worktrees were created per slice and removed only by a manual scripts/prune-worktrees.sh run; nothing removed them on merge or abandonment, and nothing refused a new one when the disk was nearly full.
- Siblings swept: prune-worktrees.sh (manual, 30 min safety model; kept as the conservative operator tool), the pulse WORKTREE_COUNT (report only), run.sh and sandbox.sh worktrees (inside user projects, not .claude/worktrees; unchanged).
- Mechanism: scripts/v10-worktree-reap.sh removes (non-force, by exact path) clean worktrees merged into origin/main or idle past 6h by HEAD reflog mtime and not on an active BOARD row; idle dirty ones are salvaged to wt-save/<name> first (node_modules and .rv deleted, loki-ts/dist discarded); detached unmerged HEADs get a wt-save ref; locked and fresh ones are kept. It runs after each main sync in train-cycle.sh and from .githooks/post-merge. scripts/v10-worktree-add.sh enforces the 40G floor (LOKI_WORKTREE_DISK_FLOOR_GB), reaping first. Pulse emits WORKTREE_SPRAWL above 20.
- Fixture: tests/test-worktree-reap.sh (merged, idle, detached, dirty-idle, .rv, dist-only, dirty-fresh, locked, fresh, BOARD-held, floor refusal, pulse 20 vs 21).
- Known limits: a locked worktree whose owning agent is dead is kept (the reaper cannot tell dead from alive, so it never touches a locked one); and slice worktrees created by the agent harness bypass the disk floor unless the dispatcher creates them through scripts/v10-worktree-add.sh.

## FC-100 The merge gate accepted a proof not bound to the PR head (MERGE-HEAD-PIN)
- User saw: a PR could merge on a VERIFIED verdict that was earned by a different commit than the one merged (last-proof-id pointed at whatever ran last, and gh pr merge named no expected head).
- Law: a verdict binds to the exact bytes it judged; the merge must prove the head it lands equals the head that was proven.
- Cause (measured): _merge_pr_gated read .loki/state/last-proof-id.txt without comparing facts.git.head_sha to the PR headRefOid, and called gh pr merge without --match-head-commit; a scan that stopped at the first matching proof let an older VERIFIED override a newer NOT_PROVEN.
- Siblings swept: verify-pr respond headRefOid compare (P12-04, loki-ts); the queue path shares _merge_pr_gated and is pinned too.
- Mechanism: the gate reads headRefOid, requires every clean (non-dirty) proof for that head to agree on VERIFIED, refuses with gate: head when none exists or the head is unreadable or moved, and merges with --match-head-commit.
- Fixture: tests/test-loki-merge-queue.sh (pin flag, stale proof, proof resolved by head, older VERIFIED vs newer NOT_PROVEN, dirty proof, unreadable head, head moved).
- Known limits: a proof without facts.git.head_sha never matches (fails closed); dirty is ignoring .loki/ only.

## FC-101 A receipt field the writer always emits was treated as optional, so deleting it passed as UNSIGNED (RECEIPT-FIELD-OPTIONAL)
- User saw: a hand-edited receipt with events_sha256, time or time.stages removed (or set to null or a string) verified as UNSIGNED, and --allow-unsigned accepted it.
- Law: a verifier may treat a field as optional only if some writer omits it; absence of an always-written field is evidence of editing, never a pass.
- Cause (measured): checkEventLog returned null when events_sha256 was not a string, and the P12-01b stages check accepted any non-object time; seal.ts has written both fields since ad80fd4e6.
- Siblings swept: the group receipt (seal_group.ts), Python proof receipts and control-plane fixtures never carry these fields and are not gated; signed receipts already cover both fields through receipt_sha256 and the attestation. The control-plane verify fixture (packages/control-plane/test/server/verify.test.ts signed()) and the undo_plan, undo_apply and workspace e2e receipt/1 fixtures are gated and now carry seal-shaped fields (empty-log hash with zero stages).
- Mechanism: verify_cmd.ts checkEventLog, for schema loki.v10.receipt/1 only, returns TAMPERED naming the missing field (events_sha256 not 64-char lowercase hex, time absent or null, time.stages not a plain object) and ends "Re-run the task to produce a fresh receipt."
- Fixture: loki-ts/tests/engine10/verify_cmd.test.ts P12-01c (field missing, malformed hash, time removed with stages at top level, time null, stages string, stages array, honest zero-stage receipt stays UNSIGNED); hand-built receipt/1 fixtures in seal_group, log_seal and visual_evidence tests now carry both fields.
- Known limits: closed by FC-103 (a schema variant or no schema field is now TAMPERED).

## FC-102 A resumed run lost intake's deferred already_satisfied flag (D61-04, P12-07j) (RESUME-ALREADY-SATISFIED)
- User saw: after `loki start --resume`, a run whose intent was already satisfied committed and verified its own diff and opened a redundant PR instead of short-circuiting to ALREADY_SATISFIED. Raw: the deferred hit was applied in memory only.
- Cause (measured): the deferred hit mutates intake's data after intake's stage.completed was emitted (machine.ts keeps the same object for that reason), so resumeMachineOpts restored intake from a payload that never had the flag.
- Law: L4 (the durable log must carry every fact a later stage reads).
- Siblings swept: every other stage output restored by resumeMachineOpts is final before its stage.completed emit; intake is the only stage mutated afterward. Readers of intake.already_satisfied: machine.ts mustJump, seal.ts verdictOf, discard.ts.
- Mechanism: the worker emits `already.satisfied` (already_done_async.ts, after hitsUnchangedFromBase and apply succeed). SupervisorLog.ingest attests it as the SUPERVISOR_ONLY event `already.attested` only for a well-formed deferred hit while implement is in flight; a worker line of type already.attested is dropped. resumeMachineOpts restores the flag only from already.attested that precedes implement's stage.completed with no later intent.revised (stale evidence). Absent, malformed or rejected means not satisfied; old logs restore as before.
- Guarantee: a restored flag jumps past verify. VERIFIED is impossible because verdictOf returns ALREADY_SATISFIED, or PARTIAL without proof, whenever intake.already_satisfied is true.
- Fixture: loki-ts/tests/engine10/fix_resume.test.ts P12-07j (survives, old log, unattested worker event, malformed, intent.revised, post-implement, ingest attestation, verdictOf); tests/engine10/already_done.test.ts (emit payload and ordering).
- Known limits: a compromised worker can still fake a well-formed already.satisfied while implement is in flight, the same claim it can already make through the LOKI_ALREADY_DONE marker; the attestation closes forgery outside that window and log edits (tamper.detected). discard.ts restores staged paths on this flag, so that window remains the trust boundary. comment and comment_argv are not restored.

## FC-103 An unrecognized or missing receipt schema skipped every schema-gated check and read UNSIGNED, and a verify test turned a failed verification into SKIP (RECEIPT-SCHEMA-UNKNOWN)
- User saw: a re-hashed unsigned receipt whose schema was renamed or removed verified as UNSIGNED (exit 0 under --allow-unsigned); tests/test-mcp-tasks-verify-readonly.sh printed SKIP and exited 0 when the signed verify returned a non-VERIFIED result with no verdict line.
- Law: a verifier dispatches on a closed set of schemas; anything outside the set is evidence of editing, never a pass. A test may skip only on a named absent precondition, never on a verification result.
- Cause (measured): schema-gated checks (FC-101, group section) were opt-in per schema, so a receipt that matched no schema matched no check; the test's catch-all case branch mapped every output without "verdict: " to SKIP.
- Siblings swept: DSSE envelope and composite.json dispatch before this point and have their own hash checks; group/1 without a group section was closed in P12-01d; the other verify-path tests (test-loki-verify-*, verify_cmd.test.ts) assert on verdicts, not on skips.
- Mechanism: verify_cmd.ts verifyReceipt returns TAMPERED naming "unrecognized receipt schema" unless schema is loki.v10.receipt/1 or loki.v10.group/1 (P12-01d, extended here by tests); the MCP verify test (classify_non_verified) fails on every non-VERIFIED result and SKIPs only when the output contains the engine message "cannot run on this machine: no working bun" (bin/loki checks PATH and the bundled @oven/bun-*, so a PATH probe is the wrong precondition).
- Fixture: loki-ts/tests/engine10/verify_cmd.test.ts P12-01d loop (renamed, removed, non-string, empty, null, case-variant); tests/test-mcp-tasks-verify-readonly.sh run with PATH lacking bun, a bundled node_modules/@oven/bun-*/bin/bun and LOKI_TS_ENTRY at a crashing stub (old: SKIP 6/0, new: FAIL) plus the classify_non_verified cases.
- Known limits: the only SKIP is the engine-reported "no working bun"; a bun that exists but crashes fails. The unsigned cases of the same script always run.

## FC-104 A steer claim linked to a normal archive was swept as "applied" although the consumer died before applying it (STEER-APPLIED-UNPROVEN)
- User saw: an operator steer vanished: never injected, never reported, and Seal passed.
- Law: "applied" must be provable from a durable record written before the evidence that could contradict it is removed; an inode match proves only that a link happened.
- Cause (measured): consumeSteer linked the claim to human-input-<stamp>.md, unlinked the claim, then recorded revisions. P12-02f's sweep removed any claim sharing an inode with a normal archive, so a kill between link and record (or unlink and record) lost the steer silently.
- Siblings swept: ORPHAN and REJECTED archives (already reported when made, still removed quietly); the no-hardlink rename fallback in archiveTo (EPERM, ENOTSUP, EXDEV, EMLINK), which now also runs the record and marker step right after the rename (no claim survives there, so the order is record after rename); a dropped call there left the sink and intent-revisions.jsonl empty and Seal passing.
- Mechanism: archiveTo runs an onLinked step before the claim unlink: revisions are recorded, then a .applied-<archive> marker is written. sweepOrphans removes a claim linked to a normal archive only when that marker exists; otherwise it archives it ORPHAN and reports "orphaned claim" so Seal refuses.
- Fixture: loki-ts/tests/e10ext/steer.test.ts (P12-02g kill-before-record, marker-before-unlink order, applied-then-unlink-failed removed quietly).
- Known limits: markers whose archive is gone are pruned by the sweep; the marker is agent-writable like the revision file; a kill between record and marker reports a recorded steer as unapplied (fails closed).
- Known limits (no-hardlink filesystems): a kill after the rename fallback and before the revision record leaves no claim to sweep, so that steer is lost unreported; the order is unchanged from P12-02f.

## FC-REQ Recovery treated a flaky pass, a protocol violation and a requirements conflict as the same transient failure (P12-11) (RECOVERY-CLASSES)
- User saw: a test that failed once and passed on rerun was reported as a failure or retried blindly; a conflicting spec was re-prompted until the budget ran out.
- Law: a class is claimed only from structured evidence, never from agent prose; an unrecognized failure stays transient and is retried (retry_class.ts fail-safe).
- Cause (measured): decideRecovery knew only permanent, transient, build_failed, tree_corrupt and provider_unavailable; supervisor.ts hardcoded `flaky: []` and nothing remembered failures across runs.
- Siblings swept: verify.ts already reruns once and marks fail-then-pass "flaky" (the evidence source); retry_class.ts classifyFailure is unchanged; the permanent-failure stop and the repeated-signature breaker still run before any new class.
- Mechanism: recovery_policy.ts adds RecoverySignals rerun (same non-empty tree on fail and pass), protocolError and conflictingRequirements. Results: flaky -> retry, protocol_error -> revise, conflicting_requirements -> escalate. util/failure_memory.ts recordFingerprints (extends the existing failure-memory owner; flaky.json and failures.jsonl keep no per-run rows) appends fingerprints to .loki/state/fingerprints.jsonl (append-only, newest 200 kept) and supervisor.ts feeds verify's flaky list into it and the summary.
- Fixture: loki-ts/tests/runner/harness_intelligence.test.ts "P12-11 recovery classes (FC-REQ)" (classifier table, fingerprints across two runs, bound).
- Known limits: protocolError and conflictingRequirements have no producer wired yet (callers must set them from structured signals); fingerprints are recorded for flaky only and not yet read back by a policy rule; verify's flaky list reaches decideRecovery as the rerun signal (P12-11b) and Q17-03 adds failedChecks so a real failure beside a flaky check still revises (failedChecks has no production producer yet, so absent data keeps flaky-wins); engine10 renderMainOutput still passes flaky: [] (names reach the user via seal notProven); the bounding rewrite is best effort and a concurrent append can lose a row.

## FC-P12-32 Router cost objective is built but dark in production (RESOLVED by P12-32b)
- User-visible vs raw: no route decision changes today; the expected accepted-outcome cost ranking (history.ts routeScore, rankExecutorsByCost, haikuFloorExecutor) has no production caller.
- Law broken: a control nobody reads is theatre (reader without a live caller).
- Cause (measured): grep of loki-ts/src finds zero callers of resolveExecutor (decision.ts) and none that sets its historyFloor input, so haikuFloorExecutor is unreachable outside tests.
- Siblings swept: decision.ts envOverride, model_rank.ts nextState and issues_epic.ts are the only decision.ts importers; none builds a ResolveInput.
- Mechanism: seal.ts recordRun now also records FAILED as a code-owned loss (cost_preview.ts outcomeFor), so acceptance is measured; the remaining step is a caller that builds ResolveInput with historyFloor = haikuFloorExecutor(...) when the router resolves the stage executor.
- Fixture: loki-ts/tests/runner/cost_preview.test.ts "P12-32 outcomeFor", router_history.test.ts P12-32 cases.
- Resolved (P12-32b): implement_route.ts routeStart passes a lazy repoHistoryFloor to unit_model.ts routeRecord, which applies history.ts historyRaisedRoute (through resolveExecutor) to a valid cheapest-tier route; history only raises, and no history, below-floor history or corrupt history gives today's routing. Fixture: router_history.test.ts "P12-32b live cost routing".
- Known limits: acceptance counts only VERIFIED and FAILED; PARTIAL runs are not recorded; history never selects a cheaper unproven model, by design.

- Siblings to sweep: every name in the modernize types.ts event list must have at least one producer in loki-ts/src; cost has no per-unit producer either (only budget.hit spent_usd at scheduler.ts:203).
- Mechanism: the coordinator (loki-ts/src/util/modernize_coordinator.ts) calls writeReport once at the end of a run; reslice.ts appends unit.resliced; a guard test asserts every declared event name has a producer.

## FC-M25W-b Modernize events unit.planned and codemod.skipped are declared with no producer (MODERNIZE-EVENT-DARK)
- User-visible vs raw: the modernize event log never shows which units were planned or why a codemod was skipped, so a user cannot see the plan or a missing-tool reason; the raw cause is two names in the types.ts event list with no append call.
- Law broken: a control nobody reads is theatre (event declared with no producer).

## FC-COST-PARTIAL A cost total built from ledger lines alone is reported as complete when the main records carry tokens but no dollar figure (COST-PARTIAL-HIDDEN)
- User-visible vs raw: `loki` cost and the C-F1 benchmark show a small total with cost_partial false, so a run looks cheaper than it was and the 2x cost claim could rest on an understated number.

- Mechanism: when main records exist and cost_available is false, set cost_partial true in the one reader; siblings consume that flag.
- Fixture: tests/test_efficiency_cost_availability.py case with token-only main record plus a priced ledger line asserts cost_partial true.

- User-visible vs raw: v11.17.8 never published; Tests run 38986362613 failed only "real-run harness scenarios, dry install, doctored receipt" with `FAIL wall-guard: expected check "node:sum.test.js" executed in checks[]`.

## FC-COST-PARTIAL-b Partial cost still readable as complete in sibling consumers (COST-PARTIAL-SIBLINGS)
- User-visible vs raw: a run whose cost is only partly measured can still be shown as a complete number in the runner equivalence report, the bench-run report, or a spend-cap message without its lower-bound label.

## FC-25c-ci Git env overrides leaked by an earlier suite silence repo-config controls (GIT-ENV-LEAK)
- User-visible vs raw: v11.17.9 never published; Tests run 38988194257 failed 2 FC-25c(a) controls on ubuntu while they passed locally per file.
- Law broken: a test must be hermetic; a control that depends on process-wide state is not a control.
- Cause (reproduced 2026-10-17): runAutonomous (autonomous.ts:448) and issues_run.ts:512 call withholdGithubTokens on process.env inside the shared bun process, leaving GIT_SSH_COMMAND=false and a GIT_CONFIG_COUNT credential.helper reset; both override repo config. Running the old file with those four variables set reproduces exactly the 2 CI failures.
- Siblings to sweep: every test that asserts a repo-local git config value runs (fsmonitor, filter, ssh, credential, uploadpack).
- Mechanism: hermeticGit wrapper in fc25_safe_git.test.ts removes GIT_SSH, GIT_SSH_COMMAND, GIT_CONFIG_PARAMETERS and GIT_CONFIG_COUNT/KEY/VALUE for the test and restores them after.
- Fixture: the same file run with GIT_SSH_COMMAND=false GIT_CONFIG_COUNT=1 GIT_CONFIG_KEY_0=credential.helper GIT_CONFIG_VALUE_0= passes 14/0.

## FC-TEN-QUALITY-01 The fix session gets a bare check name with no assertion text (FIX-BRIEF-BLIND)
- User-visible vs raw: the user saw fix rounds repeat or stall on a failing test. Raw pytest printed `E assert 4 == 5`; the fix brief carried only the check name and the rerun command.
- Law broken: L4 (the fixer must see the evidence the verifier saw).
- Siblings: every runner whose decisive block differs: pytest `E ` lines, jest/vitest Expected/Received, node AssertionError, go `--- FAIL`, tsc/ruff diagnostics; holdout checks must stay hidden.
- Mechanism: one failureExcerpt() in util/check_result.ts (redacted, repo-relative, at most 1500 bytes per check, 6 KB total), held in a stage-local map in verify.ts. It is never part of the check signature, never emitted in test.result, never computed for holdout checks.
- Fixture: fix.test.ts (failing pytest `assert add(2,2) == 5`), check_result.test.ts (one fixture per runner plus size cap and AKIA redaction), wall_holdout.test.ts (holdout text absent from failures_grouped, events and brief).

## FC-TEN-QUALITY-02 Pre-existing lint cannot be told apart from lint the change introduced (LINT-NO-BASELINE)
- User-visible vs raw: the user saw a fix loop spending rounds on lint that was already in the repo. Raw base output showed the same diagnostics before the change.
- Law broken: L4 (attribution must compare head to base, as tests already do via attribute()).
- Siblings: ruff concise, shellcheck gcc, eslint unix parsers; tsc is out of scope; a file new on head has no base.
- Mechanism: one pure attributeStatic(tool, headOut, baseOut) in runner/repo_profile.ts next to attribute() and attributeFullSuite(). Multiset keyed path/code/message (line and column dropped). Fails closed: unparseable head, empty parse with non-zero exit, missing base and unknown tool all count as introduced.

## FC-TEN-GROUP-AUTH Group child sessions lose the OS identity vars and report no cost (SCOPED-ENV-NO-IDENTITY)

The remaining child-env helpers copy the full env and remove named secrets, so they never dropped identity.
- Mechanism: ENV_KEEP in run_group.ts gains USER, LOGNAME, SHELL; parseGroupCost and groupCostLine sum the per-unit cost rows of every backlog.py launch and print one measured, partial or not-measured `Cost:` line like a single run, and writeGroupCostRecord rolls the sum into the parent .loki/metrics/efficiency so collect_efficiency, receipts and the bench read it. parseGroupCost ignores output with no Summary table. No shared base list: the allowlists differ in everything but the identity trio, so a shared list would not remove the copies.
- Fixture: tests/features/run_group_env_cost.test.ts (scopedEnv keeps USER and LOGNAME and drops an unrelated secret; group cost equals the sum of unit costs, unmeasured rows read partial, none reads not measured; a runGroup test with a fake launcher asserts the Cost line and the efficiency record); tests/runner/spawn_env_guard.test.ts stays green.

## FC-TEN-SECURITY-26 The URL userinfo redact rule restarted inside one scheme-char run, so redaction was quadratic and the Bun suite went red on slow runners (URI-SCHEME-RESTART)
- User-visible vs raw: user-visible. `redact.test.ts` "assignment redact covers flag and separator-prefixed names" failed on GitHub runners (175-220ms against a 100ms budget, main v11.17.58 run 39048066486 and PR #225 run 39122713723). The slow input was `"--a".repeat(7000) + "token"`; the TEN-SECURITY-25 assignment rule took 0.1ms on it. The URL userinfo rule `\b[a-z][a-z0-9+.-]*://` restarted at every letter after - + or . and rescanned the run: 49, 212, 847ms at N, 2N, 4N.
- Law broken: redaction runs on every event, log line and proof field, so it must stay linear in input length; a quadratic rule lets one long token stall the engine writer, shipper or proof generator.
- Siblings: the same scheme shape in loki-ts/src/util/redact.ts, loki-ts/src/council/finding_schema.ts and autonomy/lib/proof_redact.py `_URI_CREDENTIAL`, all bounded in 01e81302c. A second, Python-only quadratic survived that fix: the password class `[^\s@]+@` scanned an "@"-free tail from every scheme start (`"a://" * 16000` took 5.6s with the bounded scheme). Left as is: the TEN-SECURITY-25 ASSIGNMENT rule's `(?:[A-Za-z0-9]+[_.-]+)*` group drops off the JSC regex JIT above about 80000 iterations (`"--a".repeat(80000)` 57ms, 320000 reps 393ms); still linear, roughly 0.5us per char past the cliff, far above the sizes the suite or real log lines use.
- Mechanism: scheme bounded to 64 chars in all three rules (01e81302c). For the Python password tail, `_redact_uri_credentials` runs `_URI_CREDENTIAL` only on each whitespace token up to its last "@"; no match can end past it. Differential fuzz against the plain regex: 0 diffs over 400k random strings, redaction count included.
- Fixture: loki-ts/tests/util/redact.test.ts and tests/test_proof_redaction.py `test_assignment_redaction_is_linear_on_long_input` (scheme inputs from 01e81302c; `"a://" * 50000` and `"a://u:" * 35000` added for the password tail, red on the bounded-only source, green after).
## FC-ISSUE-FOREIGN-REPO Issue mode on a repo the user cannot push to ended in a raw 403, posted on someone else's issue, and sealed no Wall result (FOREIGN-REPO-NO-PROOF)
- User-visible vs raw: user-visible. `loki start anthropics/claude-code#21527` (a repo the operator cannot push to, no test runner) printed BLOCKED with NOT PROVEN carrying three raw lines of git stderr (`remote: Permission to anthropics/claude-code.git denied`, `fatal: ... 403`), a Reason cut mid-word at 200 chars, the intent card glued to the live line, and "wall result not recorded by verify". A no-PR rerun on the same repo sealed PARTIAL "no tests to run": the Wall wrote a bun:test file and verify never ran it.
- Law broken: a run must end in a sealed, honest result the operator can act on; a credential or permission miss must read as one line naming the cause, and Loki must not act on a third-party issue the operator does not maintain.
- Siblings: push-pr (direct push, no fork path), issue-comment status and backstop comments (posted on the foreign issue), verify (Wall tests only from the project test map; `wall_passed` never written anywhere, so every Wall receipt carried the not-recorded line), wall guessRunner (any non-py/go file mapped to the JS runner), output Reason (mid-word slice) and NOT PROVEN (newlines kept), intent card (stderr write over the supervisor's live line).
- Mechanism: engine10-push.sh fork mode (gh `permissions.push` false: push to the verified fork through `_loki_trusted_push`'s new destination arg, PR upstream from user:branch; a draft run stops at the fork, exit 4; status and backstop comments skipped, the receipt comment on the user's own PR kept; `LOKI_PR_FORK=0` off). testmap `harnessRunners`: no detected runner and no Python/Go test suite means Loki's bun runs the Wall file in the Wall and in verify (`LOKI_WALL_HARNESS_RUNNER=0` off). verify writes `wall_passed`. term_width `clipWords` and `pushErrLine` for one-line output.
- Fixture: tests/test-engine10-push.sh fork-mode block (6 cases, 5 red before), loki-ts/tests/engine10/verify.test.ts "no project runner", testmap.test.ts harnessRunners, output.test.ts clip and one-line NOT PROVEN, pr.test.ts fork-only and pushErrLine. Live: the same task on a clone of anthropics/claude-code went from verify 0s with no checks to the Wall check executed and failing for the stated reason (BLOCKED, the request lives in the Slack backend, not that repo).

## FC-NO-DIFF-PUBLISH A run with nothing to publish still pushed a branch, and its stop reason read as glued prose (NO-DIFF-PUBLISH)
- User-visible vs raw: user-visible. A rerun of `loki start anthropics/claude-code#21527` after FC-ISSUE-FOREIGN-REPO ended BLOCKED (SPEC_CONFLICT, 0 files changed) yet pushed an empty branch to the operator's fork (NOT PROVEN: "PR not opened: fork-only: pushed ..."), logged the skipped status comment as ok, spent two more fix rounds (one on opus) after the implement session had already declared the conflict, and printed a Reason with two model messages joined mid-word ("has shippedI still").
- Law broken: a run publishes only what it changed, and its result reads as one honest line; spend stops once the outcome is decided.
- Siblings: the supervisor PR gate (net diff computed only for FAILED, so SPEC_CONFLICT, PARTIAL and VERIFIED skipped it), the status projector and backstop comment (a skip read as a post), session marker parsing (LOKI_SPEC_CONFLICT and LOKI_ALREADY_DONE both line-anchored on glued text), the fix loop (E-98b keeps verifying a single conflict claim, but a fix session confirming it with an unchanged tree was not treated as confirmation).
- Mechanism: one net-diff check for every verdict gates every push; the push script reports a skipped comment on stdout and callers record it; session text blocks are newline-separated before marker parsing; a fix round that also exits spec_conflict on an unchanged tree ended the loop (superseded by FC-FIX-OVER-CONFLICT: no fix round runs at all after implement declares the conflict).
- Fixture: supervisor no-diff SPEC_CONFLICT case (no push), tests/test-engine10-push.sh skipped-comment line, session two-block marker case, machine confirmed-conflict case.

## FC-LOKI-DIR-DIRT A repo that committed Loki's own .loki/ state could never start a run once that state was removed (LOKI-DIR-DIRT)
- User-visible vs raw: user-visible. `loki start asklokesh/FireLater#20` ended FAILED in 1s with "dirty tracked tree: D .loki/CONTINUITY.md, D .loki/STATUS.txt, ...": the repo tracks four legacy-engine state files, the operator had cleared .loki/ by hand (shell history), and every later run refused to start (the same failure hit #17 on 2026-10-04).
- Law broken: Loki's own state is never the operator's change; a run is blocked only by the operator's files.
- Siblings: intake's dirty check (the only consumer of tracked-tree status before the run branch); every later stage already excludes .loki (the diff pathspec ':(exclude).loki', info/exclude, commit staging), so only intake disagreed.
- Mechanism: intake's dirtyTrackedFiles drops any status line whose paths are all under .loki/, rename sides included; the commit stage keeps excluding them, so the run never commits or reverts those deletions.
- Fixture: loki-ts/tests/engine10/intake.test.ts FC-LOKI-DIR-DIRT (red: the exact reported reason; green: intake completes and the run's commit names only README.md).

## FC-VACUOUS-WALL-GREEN A Wall test that already passed on base was sealed as ALREADY_SATISFIED for a feature the repo does not have (VACUOUS-WALL-GREEN)
- User-visible vs raw: user-visible. `loki start anthropics/claude-code#21527` (GitLab support for a hosted Slack integration) on 11.17.61 ended ALREADY_SATISFIED for $0.09 and posted "already satisfied" on the upstream issue. The Wall test it sealed asserted only things the base tree already had (5/5 pass on base, run a97b events wall base_run pass 1 fail 0), so a green-on-base result became the claim itself.
- Law broken: ALREADY_SATISFIED needs evidence the requested behavior exists; a test that passes before any change proves only that the test is weak.
- Siblings: seal verdictOf (green-on-base alone returned ALREADY_SATISFIED), discard alreadySatisfied (same three-way OR), the Wall installer (set already_satisfied on intake output, short-circuiting implement), runWall reauthor (kept: a green-on-base test is still reauthored once).
- Mechanism: green on base is recorded as `green_on_base` proof only; the claim comes from exactly two sources, intake's evidence-cited already-done check or implement's LOKI_ALREADY_DONE, and seal still requires proof for either. One rule in seal and discard.
- Fixture: loki-ts/tests/engine10/wall.test.ts green-on-base cases (red: ALREADY_SATISFIED with no claim; green: implement runs) and check_result.test.ts "verdict gate" FC-VACUOUS-WALL-GREEN case (a changed tree on a Wall green on base caps at PARTIAL with NOT PROVEN "Wall green on base: no Wall test discriminates the change"; the Wall proves only an intake or implement claim).

## FC-FIX-OVER-CONFLICT A fix round papered over a declared spec conflict with documentation for a behavior the code does not have (FIX-OVER-CONFLICT)
- User-visible vs raw: user-visible. On 11.17.62 the same #21527 run had implement declare LOKI_SPEC_CONFLICT twice (the feature lives in a hosted service, not that repo); verify then failed, fix round 1 wrote a CHANGELOG line and a README section announcing GitLab support, verify passed, and the commit was pushed to the operator's fork (run fdfc, commit 980a166) under a SPEC_CONFLICT seal.
- Law broken: once implement declares the task conflicts with the repository, spend stops and nothing may be written to satisfy the check; docs never substitute for missing code.
- Siblings: the machine fix loop (ran on any failing verify regardless of implement.exit), the 11.17.61 confirmation rule in the loop (required a second conflict claim from fix; removed), fix stage output spec_conflict (only consumer was that rule; removed), the implement/fix briefs (context.ts FIXED_RULES) and the shared stage prefix (lean_prefix.ts) that plan, wall and implement read.
- Mechanism: the fix loop condition stops when implement.exit is spec_conflict (verify still runs once, E-98b); FIXED_RULES and the stage prefix forbid docs, changelog or README text announcing behavior the code lacks and direct the agent to LOKI_SPEC_CONFLICT. The A-110/E-67 draft PR for a conflict with real implement work is kept.
- Fixture: loki-ts/tests/engine10/machine.test.ts E-98b case (one verify, no fix round; red on the old loop), implement brief and spec-first goldens re-captured.

## FC-FIX-CONFLICT-STALLED A spec conflict declared by a fix round was ignored and the run ended STALLED (FIX-CONFLICT-STALLED)
- User-visible vs raw: user-visible. `loki start asklokesh/FireLater#20` (run e10-20261019T020704Z-103b): fix round 2 ended with LOKI_SPEC_CONFLICT and no tree change; the fix stage dropped the marker, the anti-loop fired (loop.repeat) and the run ended STALLED "same failure 3 times" instead of BLOCKED with the agent's question and the `loki answer` next step.
- Law broken: an agent's explicit, evidenced report that the task conflicts with the repository ends the run as a spec conflict wherever it is made; a stall verdict never hides the question.
- Siblings: implement (the only stage that consumed the marker), the fix stage (dropped it), seal verdictOf and the receipt reason (read implement only), reasonOf and the supervisor blocked comment (read the implement event only). plan, wall and deep sessions are not told the marker exists and never consume it; swept, no change. A claim from a fix round that changed the tree is not believed (the agent did work; verify decides), so a conflict cannot escape a real failure.
- Mechanism: one rule shared with implement (util/conflict_resume.ts): a fix session that signals the conflict gets the same single correction resume (resumeAfterConflict) and, if it still signals it with the tree unchanged, the fix stage reports exit spec_conflict with the reason; the machine records it on the implement output so seal, output and the supervisor use their existing SPEC_CONFLICT path, and the fix loop stops before the anti-loop.
- Readers: conflictReasonOf (engine10/events.ts) is the one event reader (last fix reason, else implement's); output.ts, supervisor.ts, run_outputs.ts workItemBlockers, blocked_answer.ts readBlocked and slack_inbound.ts all use it. In deep mode implementLeftS may skip the correction resume; the unchanged-tree check still applies.
- Fixture: loki-ts/tests/engine10/machine.test.ts (red: stopped "stalled" and verdict not SPEC_CONFLICT; green: BLOCKED path with reason; tree-changed claim is not believed).

## FC-LINT-OOM Tests shard 0 died with "runner has received a shutdown signal" (exit 143) during ShellCheck Linting (LINT-OOM)
- User-visible vs raw: user-visible (red Tests on main for 11.17.61 and 11.17.63). Raw: no suite failed; the hosted runner was killed mid-lint.

- Mechanism: one scheduling rule in tests/run-shellcheck.sh: a file of at least LOKI_SHELLCHECK_HEAVY_LINES (default 5000) lines runs alone (pool drained first, nothing starts until it ends); the ratchet runs serially. Verdict logic unchanged (598 pass, 0 fail locally, 1m45s).
- Fixture: tests/test-shellcheck-heavy-serial.sh (fake shellcheck records overlap; red on the previous script, green now).

## FC-TEST-LEAKS-INTO-REPO Running the engine10 tests left an untracked loki-ts/calc.ts in the checkout (TEST-LEAKS-INTO-REPO)
- User-visible vs raw: raw (a dirty tree after any full `bun test tests/engine10`; seen in main and in engineers' worktrees). No user run is affected.
- Law broken: a test must not write outside its temp repo; a probe must not run in the caller checkout.
- Writer: preflight's codex login probe (providerLoginIssue, `codex login status`) spawned the stub codex CLI in the process cwd (loki-ts); the stub ignored its args and appended multiply() to calc.ts. Only tests/engine10/providers.test.ts reaches it. Not a session write: sessions already run in ctx.repoDir.
- Siblings: the cline and aider stubs have no probe today (they only warn on login); the e2e claude stub answers `--help` and `auth status` before touching files.
- Mechanism: the codex stub exits 0 for `login ...` before any edit, and providerLoginIssue runs the probe in tmpdir() so no probe ever executes in the caller checkout.
- Fixture: loki-ts/tests/engine10/providers.test.ts snapshots untracked files under loki-ts before and after each provider run and expects none new (red on the old stub, green now).

## FC-STACKED-ON-LOKI-RUN A new run started on an earlier unaccepted Loki run branch inherits that run's output (FC-15 family)
- User saw: `loki start anthropics/claude-code#21527` in a checkout still on loki/e10-20261018T205304Z-fdfc, the output of an earlier run sealed BLOCKED (spec conflict). The start line said `PR target: origin/main, base: loki/e10-20261018T205304Z-fdfc`; the new run branched from that HEAD, so its branch, its HANDOFF Accept line and any PR carried the earlier run's rejected commit (980a166, fabricated CHANGELOG and README lines). The acceptance "CHANGELOG.md stays unmodified" was vacuously true because the fabrication sat in the base. Raw: the raw arm starts from the user's tree and has no such commit.
- Law: L4 (the base is a contract), L2 (trust fails closed: unreviewed, rejected output is not a base), L5 (an honest line names what was skipped).
- Cause: intake.ts took base_sha, tree and the new branch from HEAD unconditionally. FC-15 only voided an ALREADY_SATISFIED claim resting on such commits; it never stopped the run building on them.
- Rule: when every commit in <PR target>..HEAD carries the Loki-Run trailer, the run branches from the PR target tip instead (the target tip rather than the merge-base, so a squash-merged run still finds its work on the target). The old branch is untouched. One line names the skipped branch and commit count; it is recorded as intake data stacked_loki_skip and shown through noteOf in the summary and PR body. Any commit without the trailer (the operator built on it deliberately), an empty range or an unresolved target keeps base = HEAD.
- Siblings swept (known follow-up: group runs, where the group baseRef stays HEAD while its units skip; the predicate never fires off a loki/e10-* run branch, so not on main after a fast-forward accept, not on loki/epic-*, loki/group-*, loki/unit-* branches, and not when LOKI_PR_BASE is set by a stacked slice of `loki issues run`): unmergedEvidence/lokiOwn (still reports the mixed Loki plus operator range); the supervisor start line (baseLine now calls the same predicate, so it agrees with intake); ctx.baseSha (set before intake from HEAD; intake now updates it, which the Wall base run, verify, seal and the PR stage read); base_sha, the tree cache key and the contract snapshot (all taken after the checkout).
- Mechanism: one predicate `stackedOnLokiRun` in loki-ts/src/util/base_guard.ts, used by intake.ts and baseLine. No new knob.
- Fixture: loki-ts/tests/engine10/intake.test.ts describe "FC-STACKED-ON-LOKI-RUN" (all-Loki range branches from origin/main with the old branch intact and the note recorded; an operator commit in the range keeps HEAD; an unresolved target keeps HEAD) plus the FC-15 case D updated to the skip.

## FC-LAZY-CONTROL-ENV Lazy-fetch control silenced by ambient git env or a racily clean index (GIT-ENV-LEAK, sibling of FC-25c-ci)
- User-visible vs raw: Tests run 39174561642 (main 8cc81a1b8) failed shard 6/8 on tests/test-untracked-status-fsmonitor.sh with "control: plain git status did not run the lazy-fetch hook MARKER=no"; the same file passed an hour earlier and 5 of 5 locally.
- Law broken: a test must be hermetic; a control that depends on inherited process env is not a control.
- Cause (reproduced 2026-10-18, git 2.55.0): GIT_SSH_COMMAND (autonomy/run.sh:5868 exports GIT_SSH_COMMAND=false) or GIT_NO_LAZY_FETCH in the inherited env makes the plain `git status` skip core.sshCommand, so the hook marker is never written. `env GIT_SSH_COMMAND=false bash tests/test-untracked-status-fsmonitor.sh` and `env GIT_NO_LAZY_FETCH=1 ...` each fail the control exactly as in CI. A second cause remained in a clean env: 1 of 20 runs failed the control because `git status` re-hashes f.txt (and so lazy-fetches the attributes blob) only when the index entry is racily clean; otherwise stat matches and no fetch happens.
- Siblings: both lazy-fetch fixtures in the file (run_lazy_case and the filter-case repo); any test asserting a repo-local ssh, fsmonitor or filter command runs.
- Mechanism: both fixtures unset GIT_SSH, GIT_SSH_COMMAND, GIT_NO_LAZY_FETCH, GIT_CONFIG_COUNT, GIT_CONFIG_PARAMETERS, GIT_DIR, GIT_WORK_TREE and set GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM=1 GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL=/dev/null, and run_lazy_case sets f.txt mtime to 2030 (as run_eq_case already does) so status always re-hashes. The control runs first; a dead control is a FAIL that marks the real leg NOT PROVEN, never a skip or pass. The real assertion is unchanged.
- Fixture: the same file passes 21/0 on 40 consecutive clean runs (was 19/1 once in 20) and with GIT_SSH_COMMAND=false GIT_NO_LAZY_FETCH=1 set.

## FC-BASELINE-PER-PACKAGE A full suite already red on base was blocked as a regression because the baseline ran a different command (BASELINE-PER-PACKAGE)
- User-visible vs raw: user-visible. `loki start asklokesh/FireLater#20` (run e10-20261018T205157Z-678e) ended FAILED with "full suite: frontend failed" after $2.60: verify ran one full suite per Project Model package, `(cd frontend && npm test)`, while the intake baseline had run only the repo's first test command at the root, so attributeFullSuite found no baseline for that command and excused nothing; vitest's per-test cross line also carried its duration ("5ms"), so even a matching command would give different ids on base and head.
- Law broken: a failure the change did not cause is never charged to the change; base and head are measured with the same command and the same id parser.
- Siblings: the legacy per-runner full suite (FULL_SUITE_CMD, `npm test --silent` vs the profile's `npm test`), the multi-root package suites (runPackageSuites), every runner whose failure line carries a timing suffix (failIds), and the REPOMEM reuse path (a new HEAD drops the per-command entries, so they are re-recorded).
- Mechanism: package_suite.ts fullSuiteSpecs is the one list of full-suite commands; verify runs it and intake's baselineSuites records a baseline per command in its own cwd (shared budget, a suite past it is recorded not run); attributeFullSuite matches each failing check to its own command's baseline and stays fail-closed (no baseline for a command, an unattributable one, or no known ids: nothing excused); failIds strips a trailing duration ("5ms", "1.2s") from FAIL and cross lines.
- Fixture: loki-ts/tests/runner/harness_intelligence.test.ts "baselineSuites records one entry per full-suite command" and "attributeFullSuite matches each package check to its own suite baseline"; loki-ts/tests/engine10/verify_prered.test.ts FC-BASELINE-PER-PACKAGE vitest duration case.

## FC-DEPS-NOT-INSTALLED A fresh clone ran its tests with no dependencies installed, and the runner's "missing packages" error read as the code's failure (DEPS-NOT-INSTALLED)
- User-visible vs raw: raw. In the same FireLater#20 run every package declared an install command (`npm ci`, citing its package-lock.json) but nothing was installed, so the Wall base run and the baseline ran against an empty dependency tree and npx printed "npm error npx canceled due to missing packages and no YES option"; that line matched no load pattern, so it was a test failure owned by the code and spent fix rounds.
- Law broken: the harness prepares the environment the repo declares before it measures anything; an environment failure is the harness's, never a fix round.
- Siblings: the FC-22b install library (prepareDeps) existed but was never wired; the runner load classifier (npm and npx missing packages, POSIX sh "N: tool: not found" next to the bash "command not found" already covered); every ecosystem whose install command the Project Model cites (decided by git, not by a list of dependency directory names).
- Mechanism: install.ts absentDepRoots names a model package whose install command cites an existing manifest or lockfile in its root while git shows no untracked or ignored directory there (Loki's own .loki/ excluded); intakeDeps runs prepareDeps for those roots once per run, after the profile's first main baseline (measured on the bare clone) but before the Wall base run; after any successful install the main baseline and every per-command suite baseline are re-run, so none is left measured without dependencies; whatever the install created untracked (node_modules without a gitignore, lockfiles, .venv) is recorded in intake as install_created (a content hash per file, created directories) and never written to git config or exclude files, so an untouched install artifact stays out of verify changedFiles and the commit while an agent-edited lockfile still counts as a change; it emits deps.install per package, and a failed install becomes deps_not_proven, which verify carries into not_proven while the run goes on; the intake stage limit grows by the install and suite-baseline caps; runner_errors.ts LOAD_PATTERNS classify both missing-package forms as a harness-owned load error, anchored at the start of a line so test output quoting them stays a test failure.
- Residual risk: source shipped inside an install-created directory (for example a new build/ or vendor/) is not committed, and a symlink to a directory is not recorded.
- Fixture: loki-ts/tests/engine10/fc22_install.test.ts "absentDepRoots and intakeDeps (FC-DEPS-NOT-INSTALLED)"; loki-ts/tests/engine10/runner_errors.test.ts FC-DEPS-NOT-INSTALLED case.
- Budget change: the intake stage limit is now STAGE_BUDGETS.intake.limitS + INSTALL_CAP_MS/1000 + SUITE_BASELINE_BUDGET_S (120s), so a repo that needs an install or a per-command baseline gets that extra time at intake; a repo with nothing absent and no suites spends none of it.
- Hardening (D106, founder 2026-10-19): the intake install is the lockfile-frozen form when a lockfile exists (install.ts lockedInstall, one table for npm, pnpm, yarn, bun, uv, poetry, pipenv, cargo, bundler, composer), fails closed as harness-owned NOT PROVEN with no mutating fallback, and records `locked`, `lock_note` and `model_cmd` on the check. Fixture: fc22_install.test.ts "lockedInstall (locked, reproducible install)".


## FC-ZERO-ON-BASE An impacted test that is a deliberate skip on base too was listed as NOT PROVEN for the change (ZERO-ON-BASE)
- User-visible vs raw: user-visible. In FireLater#20 run e10-20261019T020704Z-103b verify selected `backend/tests/integration/request-approval-race.test.ts`, a deliberate `describe.skip` (needs a real DB), so it executed zero tests and the run carried "not run: vitest:backend/tests/integration/request-approval-race.test.ts" in NOT PROVEN although the change never touched it and the pristine base executes zero tests there too.
- Law broken: a result the change did not cause is never charged to the change (the same base-comparison contract as A-112); zero-executed stays fail-closed whenever it could be about the change.
- Siblings: any runner whose file-level skip, todo or empty file yields "no tests executed" (vitest, jest, pytest, node:test, go); Wall, task-named (relevant), holdout and edited test files, and a run that edited test configuration, are never exempt; the package and full-suite not_run lines (a suite is a command, not a file) keep their own handling.
- Mechanism: verify.ts subtractBase (the A-112 pristine detached base worktree) reruns an untouched, non-Wall, non-relevant zero-executed check once and classifies it with the same classifyCheck; zero on base too puts the name in `skipped_on_base` (verify data, plus a `test.skipped_on_base` event "skipped on base: <check>"), and seal omits only those names from its "not run:" NOT PROVEN lines. Any other base outcome (tests ran, failed, missing, timed out) keeps NOT PROVEN. The rerun happens only in this case, and the check's installed node_modules or .venv are linked into the base worktree, never copied.
- Fixture: loki-ts/tests/engine10/verify_prered.test.ts describe "FC-ZERO-ON-BASE" (untouched skip listed; edited file, Wall file and tests-ran-on-base all stay fail-closed) and loki-ts/tests/engine10/seal.test.ts "FC-ZERO-ON-BASE".
- Hardening (HIGH review of 4e449d487): linking the head checkout's node_modules into the base worktree lets a workspace link (node_modules/shared -> ../shared, pnpm/npm/yarn workspaces) or an editable install (.venv with pip -e) resolve to HEAD sources, so the base rerun could execute the run's own edits and report a false zero-on-base. Choice: the excusal applies only when the run changed nothing but test files (`changed.every(isTestFile)`) and no test configuration, so no head source, manifest or lockfile exists for any link to expose; deps stay linked because base has none of its own. A run that edits any source keeps zero-executed as NOT PROVEN. Residual: edits made inside a gitignored node_modules or .venv are not in `changed`. Seal also writes one informational line "skipped on base (excused): <check>" next to the retained pre red lines (never downgrades). Regression fixture: verify_prered.test.ts "workspace link" (red before the rule).
- Hardening 2 (HIGH re-review of 9a4267335, D99): a changed TEST file can also be reached through a workspace link (node_modules/shared/helper.test.js), so test-files-only is not enough. depsSeeHead (util/base_guard.ts) withholds the excusal whenever the dependency dirs the base rerun would link can see head sources: any top-level or @scope/* node_modules entry that is a symlink whose realpath is inside the repo, or a .venv holding a .pth, *.egg-link or direct_url.json that names the repo; an unreadable dependency dir counts as seeing head. This gate is in addition to the test-files-only gate. Fixture: verify_prered.test.ts "reviewer repro 2" (red before the guard).
- Hardening 3 (round 3 HIGH review, D99): depsSeeHead now walks node_modules and .venv recursively without following symlinks (so node_modules/foo/node_modules/shared -> ../../../shared is seen), inspects every symlink target, and withholds when a target is inside the repo but outside the dependency dir it lives in (an npm .bin link or a pnpm store link stays self-contained). The repo is compared as given and as realpath (/var vs /private/var, a symlinked repo dir). Editable-install files (.pth, .egg-link, direct_url.json with its file:// URL decoded by fileURLToPath so %20 matches, and __editable___*_finder.py) are checked for the repo path, each named path also by realpath. The walk is bounded at 50000 entries; exceeding it or any read error other than a missing dir withholds. The helper logic lives in util/base_guard.ts (zeroOnBase, depsSeeHead), outside the engine core cap. A pnpm workspace package links to a path inside the repo but outside node_modules, so workspace pnpm repos are withheld; that is fail-safe, and the excusal is effectively inert wherever workspace links or editable installs exist.
- Stated plainly: the motivating incident (FireLater#20, a run that edits source) stays NOT PROVEN by design. The excusal only fires for runs that changed test files alone with dependencies that cannot see head; Seal accuracy outranks delivered accuracy.
- Superseded 2026-10-19 (founder principle: a PARTIAL caused by the harness, not by missing proof, is a bug) by FC-REPO-SKIPPED-TEST-DOWNGRADES: source-editing runs are now eligible; the dependency-visibility gate (depsSeeHead) and the no-test-config-edit gate remain.

## FC-REPO-SKIPPED-TEST-DOWNGRADES A relevant test the repo itself skips (zero tests on base and head, file untouched) downgraded the verdict to PARTIAL
- User-visible vs raw: user-visible. FireLater#20 run e10-20261019T171919Z-cc1c sealed PARTIAL with every executed check green: verify selected `backend/tests/integration/request-approval-race.test.ts` (wholly `describe.skip`, needs a real DB, unchanged by the run), it executed zero tests (events.jsonl seq 60, result not_run, reason "no tests executed (ran 0 tests, empty or all skipped)"), and verdictOf returns PARTIAL for any check whose result is not pass. FC-ZERO-ON-BASE had already removed the "not run:" note for such a check but (a) never excused it from the verdict, (b) never fired for a task-named (relevant) test, and (c) never fired when the run edited any non-test file.
- Law broken: a result the change did not cause is never charged to the change (A-112 base-comparison contract); and a harness-caused gap is a bug, not a missing proof (founder principle 2026-10-19). Zero-executed stays fail-closed whenever it could be about the change.
- Siblings swept: every runner that maps "ran 0 tests" to not_run goes through classifyCheck (util/check_result.ts) and so through the one zeroOnBase base rerun: vitest, jest, pytest, node:test, bun, go test, cargo. Runners whose zero is not measurable on base (no runner output parsed, base cut or missing) never excuse. Package and full-suite not_run lines (a suite is a command, not a file) are unchanged and still downgrade.
- Mechanism (one, extended): verify.ts subtractBase candidate filter drops its `!rel.has` exclusion (a relevant, non-Wall, non-holdout, untouched not_run with the NO_TESTS_REASON is measured on the pristine base like any other), and `zeroOk` no longer requires the run to have changed test files only (it keeps: no test configuration edit; zeroOnBase still withholds whenever the linked dependency dirs can see head, depsSeeHead). Zero on base too puts the name in `skipped_on_base`. seal.ts then (1) leaves exactly those not_run checks out of the verdictOf input (as pre_red fails already are), (2) records each in the receipt with `repo_skipped: true` and result not_run (never dropped), (3) writes the non-blocking note "skipped by the repo on base and head: <check>". Guards kept in verdictOf: the run still needs an executed passing n>0 check (FC-16 proof) and, after a limit kill, targetProofOf (n>0 pass of a Wall or relevant test): a skipped target is never a proof. Wall and holdout checks are never candidates in verify (they are excluded from the zero-candidate filter); seal additionally refuses a skipped_on_base name that matches a Wall file path (equal to the path after the runner prefix, or ending with / plus it). It does not cross-check target_checks: a relevant (task-named) test is eligible by design, and a skipped one is never a proof because target_checks lists only passing checks.
- Intake zero baseline (replaces the manifest-text gate and the environment fingerprint, both bypassed by content-level edits: remove optdep and uninstall it, move the line between dependency blocks, delete node_modules/optdep/index.js keeping its package.json; the base rerun links HEAD's node_modules and .venv, so any environment edit the run makes is invisible to it): a zero check is excused ONLY when the same check was also zero at INTAKE, on the base commit in an environment the agent had not touched (REPOMEM pattern). Measured on the real FireLater#20 artifact: the intake baseline recorded only `npm test` failing ids (unattributable) and per-package full suites, never per-file results, so the check was not covered; intake now calls probeZeroChecks (verify.ts, one run of each detected test file, bounded by 300 files and 120 s, never throws) and records `zero_baseline: {measured, zero}`. verify excuses a name only if it is in `zero`; a check not measured there (over the bound, cut, missing, absent field) is withheld. The base rerun (zeroOnBase) stays as a second, stricter requirement. depsChangedBeyondAdditions, depsFingerprint and depsEnvKept were deleted: under the baseline rule they add no protection (a pre-change measurement is immune to later edits). Residual: a repo with more than 300 test files only excuses the probed ones. Fixtures: verify_prered.test.ts removed dependency, BYPASS1, BYPASS2, BYPASS3, check absent from the baseline, missing baseline (negatives), FireLater#20-shaped additions-only run, probeZeroChecks.
- Residual: a dependency that changes how an unchanged file registers tests would need a symlink into head (withheld) or a config edit (withheld). Base not measured (no base worktree, cut, missing) keeps today's behavior.
- Fixture: loki-ts/tests/engine10/verify_prered.test.ts describe "FC-REPO-SKIPPED-TEST-DOWNGRADES" (relevant skip with source edit is skipped_on_base; diff-touched file, Wall file, zero-on-head-but-ran-on-base, test-config edit stay fail-closed) and loki-ts/tests/engine10/seal.test.ts "FC-REPO-SKIPPED-TEST-DOWNGRADES" (verdict VERIFIED with receipt repo_skipped and note; unlisted not_run, no executed proof, Wall name and base-not-measured all still PARTIAL).
- Separate finding (no code change): bare "council" and "security scan" in the NOT PROVEN of that run are the high-tier deep items (check_result.ts deepGaps). They never enter verdictOf (seal adds them to notes after the verdict is computed). They did not run because the fast engine defers them to the detached post-PR deep verify (supervisor.ts, spawned only after pr.opened); this run had pr_url null (no pr.opened or deep.started event), so they could not run. Not a verdict cause here; tracked as an observation that a PARTIAL run never gets its deep verify.
## FC-FULLSUITE-ANSI-IDS A repo whose full suite was already red on base ended FAILED because the head full-suite run parsed no failure ids from colored output (FULLSUITE-ANSI-IDS)
- User-visible vs raw: user-visible. FireLater#20 on loki 11.17.74: a frontend vitest suite with 307 known failing ids in `.loki/state/repo-profile.json` ended FAILED "full suite: frontend failed". Raw: the head run's output was colored, `failIds` returned [] (stripped, the same output yields 296 ids, all inside the base set), and attributeFullSuite treats ids=[] as "introduced (blocking)". The fix brief also carried only the command, so the fixer saw no failure text.
- Law: a parser reads what the tool said, not how it was painted (FC-69); a user's environment must not change a verdict; unknown never excuses, but the reason it is unknown must be named.
- Cause (measured): src/project_model/package_suite.ts runPackageSuites and the legacy loop in engine10/stages/deep.ts runFullSuite ran the suite with plain process.env (no plainTestEnv) and passed raw output to failIds without stripAnsi. The baseline writer (runner/repo_profile.ts shellRunner) already used both, so base had ids and head had none. Unswept sibling of FC-69.
- Siblings swept (every `failIds(` and test-output-parsing `run(` under loki-ts/src, all checked): package_suite.ts runPackageSuites (fixed), engine10/stages/deep.ts runFullSuite (fixed), runner/repo_profile.ts shellRunner (already plainTestEnv + stripAnsi), engine10/stages/verify.ts runOnce (plainTestEnv + stripAnsi, FC-69) and its base rerun failIds at subtractBase (consumes runOnce output), runner/load_owner.ts runOnBase (plainTestEnv + stripAnsi), engine10/stages/wall.ts (plainTestEnv + stripAnsi), runner/quality_gates.ts npm test run (summaryFailedCount strips ANSI itself; no ids parsed). The remaining `run(` sites (python, doctor, proof, status, provider, supply, speed) do not parse test output.
- Mechanism: one `suiteEnv(path)` (plainTestEnv plus the caller's PATH override) for both full-suite runners; ANSI stripped at capture before harnessLoadReason, classifyCheck and failIds; `failIds` strips ANSI itself (idempotent) so no caller can regress; attributeFullSuite says "head failure ids unparseable" instead of "none of the failing checks match them" when a failing check has no parsed ids against an attributable baseline (still blocking, fail-closed); a full-suite failure carries a redacted bounded `failureExcerpt` into the existing verify `excerpts` map, so the fix brief shows failure text.
